Pseudo:

```
def seq_to_fft(a, N=None):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    if N is None: N=1<<(3*n-2).bit_length()
    return np.fft.rfft(a, N), N
```
For each iteration, Ngrad power of two. Precompute index `grad_idx = 2*n-2 - np.arange(n)`.

`compute_c_and_grad(a,N,grad_idx,q)`:
```
F = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
C = np.fft.irfft(F*F, N)[:L]
C = 0.5*(C + C[::-1]) # sym
C = np.maximum(C,0)
M = C.max()
if q == 0: W = np.ones(L)
else:
  cpos=np.maximum(C,1e-300)
  logw=q*np.log(cpos); logw-=logw.max(); W=np.exp(logw)
# W symmetric? C sym, log sym, W sym. Normalize
s=W.sum(); if s==0: W=np.zeros(L); W[n-1]=1 #? but uniform handled
else W/=s
Wr = W[::-1]
G = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(Wr, N) * F, N) # length N, first 3n-2 valid? Actually F length N from a zero padded N; Wr length L padded N. N>=3n-2. Yes.
grad = G[2*n-2 - np.arange(n)]  # no factor 2
return C,M,W,grad
```
Wait `F` computed with N large; F*F irfft gives full conv length L zero-padded valid. Good.
But `G` as irfft of F*Wf gives circular convolution of a and Wr length N. Since N >= n+L-1, first len=3n-2 valid. However `G` may be complex tiny imaginary; use `.real`. `np.fft.irfft` returns real. It gives full circular, but since zero-padded enough, first 3n-2 = full conv. Good.

Symmetric optimizer:
Given b length m. a = symmetric(b). Need compute gradient wrt a, then grad_b:
```
g_b = g_a copy for first m
if n odd: m=(n+1)//2; a = [b0..b_{m-1}, b_{m-2}..b0]
g_b[i] = g_a[i]
if i<m-1: g_b[i] += g_a[n-1-i]
if even: m=n//2; a=[b0..b_{m-1}, b_{m-1}..b0]
g_b[i]=g_a[i]+g_a[n-1-i]
```
Then center g_b mean? For scale invariance of a: scaling b scales a; gradient of scaled-invariant objective should satisfy ∑ g_b b? In log descent, adding constant to g_b invariant. Subtract mean.
Update b via mirror:
```
logb=log(b+eps)-eta*g_b/scale
b_new=exp(logb); b_new/=sum
a=symmetric(b_new)
a/=a.sum()
```
Need ensure a sum1 automatically if b sum1? If b sum1 and symmetric, a sum1 (odd/ even). Good.

```

def spectral_start(n, rng, iters=200):
    # start with window (e.g. Hann) or random nonneg normalized
    a = one of windows
```

```
import time, math
import numpy as np

def normalize_nonneg(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    x=np.maximum(x,0.0)
    s=x.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.ones_like(x)
    return x/s
```

```
def compute_convolution(a, N, L):

 c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)[:L]
 c = 0.5*(c + c[::-1])
 c = np.maximum(c,0.0)

 return c, F
```

```
def active_weights(c, M, K):
    L=len(c)
    K = int(min(L, max(1,K)))
    if K == L:
       gaps=np.maximum(M-c,1e-15)
       w=1/gaps
    else:

       w=np.zeros(L)
       tops=c[idx]
       gaps=M - tops
       # floor: maybe 1e-8*(M+1)

       w[idx]=1.0/(gaps+floor)

       w=np.zeros(L); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1
       return w
```

```
def smooth_weights(c,M,q):
  # c^q
  cpos=np.maximum(c,1e-300)
  logw=q*np.log(cpos); logw-=logw.max(); w=np.exp(logw); sym; normalize
```

Gradient:
```
def gradient_from_weights(W, F, info):
    Wr = W[::-1]
    G = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(Wr, info.Ngrad) * F, info.Ngrad)
    return G[info.grad_idx].copy()
```

Mirror step:
Full:
```
def mirror_update_full(a, grad, eta):
   # a sum1 nonneg
   gd = grad - np.mean(grad)
   span = gd.max() - gd.min()
   if span <= 1e-30: return a
   loga = np.log(a + 1e-300)
   loga -= eta * gd / span
   loga -= loga.max()
   b = np.exp(loga)
   b = np.maximum(b,0.0)
   s=b.sum()
   if s<=0: return a
   return b/s
```
For symmetric:
```
def build_a_sym(b, n):
   m=len(b)
   if n%2==1:
      a=np.empty(n); a[:m]=b; a[m:]=b[-2::-1]
   else:
      a=np.empty(n); a[:m]=b; a[m:]=b[::-1]
   return a
```

```
def mirror_update_sym(b, grad_b, eta):
   gd = grad_b - np.mean(grad_b)
   span=gd.max()-gd.min()
   if span<=1e-30: return b
   logb=np.log(b+1e-300) - eta*gd/span
   logb-=logb.max()
   bnew=np.exp(logb)
   s=bnew.sum(); if s<=0 return b
   return bnew/s
```

Perturbation function:
```
def perturb_variable(var, n, rng, strength):
  if var is symmetric b:
    # add smooth random: low-pass random
    m=len(var)
    t=np.linspace(0,1,m)
    freqs = rng.integers(1,5,3)
    deltas = np.zeros(m)
    for f in freqs: deltas += rng.uniform(-1,1)*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*t + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
    var_new = var * (1 + strength*deltas)
    # also random endpoint boost?
  else:
    similar length n
  var_new=np.maximum(var_new,0)
  s=var_new.sum(); if s<...: var_new=var
  return var_new/s
```
Need no lambdas, use for loop.
Strength small 0.1. If var has zeros, multiplication can keep zeros. Good.
Could also do "swap mass" between random halves:
```
if full: i,j random; amt=var[i]*0.1; move to j; normalize
```

```
def pair_polish(a, c, M, info, deadline, rng, passes=20, top_d=20, top_r=20):
   n=len(a); L=info.L
   for pass in range(passes):
      if time>deadline: break
      # recompute c? passed
      # weights top K maybe for donor/receiver
      K = max(64, L//10)
      W=active_weights(c,M,K, alpha=0?) maybe use c weights? For donor, use smooth/active. Simpler: top lags top 20; gaps; w active. Compute grad direct for top lags? Use dense gradient? We can compute grad using W via FFT but pair polish maybe few passes; okay.
      # Or direct: grad = sum over top lag weights of shifted a. Use `make_weights` + `gradient_from_weights` requires F. We can compute F with Ngrad.
      F = rfft(a,Ngrad)
      W = make_weights(c,M,K,0.2)
      grad = gradient_from_weights(W,F,info)  # no factor 2
      # donors: top grad, receivers: bottom grad
      d_idx = np.argpartition(grad, -top_d)[-top_d:]
      r_idx = np.argpartition(grad, top_r)  # lowest
      # maybe random 20
      tried=set()
      improved=False
      for i in donor_list:
        for j in receiver_list:
          if i==j: continue
          # Skip if no mass donor in one direction; we'll try both signs so need either mass
          if a[i]+a[j]<=eps: continue
          lin=np.zeros(L)
          lin[j:j+n] += 2.0*a
          lin[i:i+n] -= 2.0*a
          # candidates
          best_delta=0; best_val=M
          ai=a[i]; aj=a[j]
          for frac in fracs:
             # i->j
             d=frac*ai
             if d>1e-18:
                 val = evaluate_delta(lin,d,2*i,2*j,i+j,c,M)
                 if val<best_val: ...
             d=-frac*aj # j->i
             if d<-1e-18:
                 val=...
          if best_val < M - tol:
             apply, update a,c,M,best improved True
      if not improved and pass>5:
          # perturb a slightly? or break
          break
   return a,c,M
```

Let's detail `optimize` early stop:
```
def optimize_variable(a, n, mode, time_budget, deadline, rng, verbose=False, no_improve_limit=60.0):
   t0=time.monotonic()
   cur...
   best...
   last_improve = t0
   while time.monotonic() < min(deadline, t0+budget):
      ...
      if M < best_M: best... last_improve=time
      if time - last_improve > no_improve_limit:
          # if we have made some progress but now stuck, maybe still perturb; after 2* limit stop
          if time - last_improve > 2*no_improve_limit: break
```

```
def make_basis(n, d):
 x=...
 cols=[]
 for k in range(d): raw = cheb(k,x) or x**k
 v=raw.copy()
 for u in cols: v -= np.dot(u,v)*u
 norm=np.linalg.norm(v); if norm<1e-9: continue; v/=norm; cols.append(v)
 B=np.column_stack(cols)
 return B
```

```
def cheb_raw(n, d):
 x=(2*arange(n)/(n-1)-1)
 V=[]
 p0=np.ones(n); V.append(p0)
 if d>0: V.append(x.copy())
 for k in range(2,d+1):
    p=2*x*V[-1]-V[-2]
    V.append(p)
 return np.column_stack(V[:d+1])
```

Let's test mentally key formulas:
`compute_C_F`: For F= rfft(a, Ngrad). `C=irfft(F*F, Ngrad)[:L]`. This gives linear convolution of a with a? Since N>=2n-1, yes. `F*F` not `F*conj(F)` because convolution not power spectrum. Good.
Gradient formula: Need verify with weighted sum W over C. Let W normalized. G = irfft(F * rfft(W_rev,Ngrad)). Index grad[j]=G[2n-2 - j]. Derivative of ∑ W_k C_k wrt a_j = 2 ∑_k W_k a_{k-j}. Our G is convolution a * W_rev: G[p]=∑_t a_t W_rev[p-t] =∑_t a_t W[ L-1 - (p-t)] = ∑_t a_t W[t+L-1-p]. Set p=L-1-j=2n-2-j. Then W[t+2n-2-(2n-2-j)] = W[t+j]. Sum_t a_t W[t+j]. Good. No factor 2. Good.
If W not symmetric, gradient correct. We sym W maybe okay. If W sym, gradient symmetric? For full mode, if a not sym, grad not sym.
Mirror update centering: For scale invariance, adding constant to gradient leaves log update same after normalization. We subtract mean (uniform) not E_a? For full variable a on simplex, adding constant to G yields derivative zero for δ with sum0? But mirror update a_new_i = a_i exp(-η G_i)/S. If G_i' = G_i + c, S' = S e^{-η c}, a unchanged. Yes, subtract any constant. Mean works.
Sym b update: Adding constant to G_b leaves b unchanged. Good.
But if b sum normalized, build a and normalize. Adding constant to G_b leaves b unchanged; a unchanged. Good.

Active weights: If K large, top indices maybe not symmetric; sym W. But if K=1, top one maybe at edge? Sym W gives pair; okay. If top includes center symmetric to itself.
`active_weights` using `np.argpartition(c, -K)` with K maybe L. If K==L, no partition. If K< L but K close, argpartition ok. Need handle K=0.
Floor: `floor = 1e-12 * max(1.0, M)`? M ~1e-4, floor 1e-16? We want gaps small. Maybe `floor = max(1e-15, 1e-6*M)`. If M=1e-4, floor 1e-10. This smooths top gap less than 1e-10 (relative 1e-6). Good. If gaps around 1e-5, weight 1e5; okay. Normalize.
Smooth weights: `Ws = c.copy()` then normalize. Add alpha. If c has zero, fine.

Functions:

```
def _safe_norm(x):
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    s = x.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        x = np.ones_like(x)
        return x / x.size
    return x / s
```

`make_info(n)`:
```
L=2*n-1
Nscore=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
size=3*n-2
Ngrad=1 << (size-1).bit_length()
idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
idx = 2*n-2 - idx
return {'L':L,'Nscore':Nscore,'Ngrad':Ngrad,'idx':idx}
```
Dict okay. Could use tuple for speed: `return L,Nscore,Ngrad,idx`. Let's use tuple.

`compute_C_F(a,N,L)`:
```
F=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
C=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)[:L]
C = 0.5*(C + C[::-1])
# remove tiny negatives
np.maximum(C, 0.0, out=C)
return C,F
```
`max_M(a,L,Nscore)`:
```
F=np.fft.rfft(a,Nscore)
C=np.fft.irfft(F*F,Nscore)[:L]
return float(np.max(C))
```
For symmetric or full, a 1D.

`make_weights(C,M,K,smooth_alpha)`:
```
L=C.size

if K<=0: K=1
K=min(K,L)
W=np.zeros(L)
if K==L:
   idx=np.arange(L)
else:
   idx=np.argpartition(C, -K)[-K:]
gaps=M-C[idx]
floor=max(1e-15, 1e-8*M)
W[idx]=1.0/(gaps+floor)
s=W.sum()
if not finite or s<=0: W[idx[0]]...? if K==L idx array.
else W/=s
# sym
W=0.5*(W+W[::-1])
s=W.sum(); if s>0 W/=s
if smooth_alpha>0:
   S=C.copy(); s=S.sum(); if s<=0 S fill? else S/=s
   S=0.5*(S+S[::-1]); s=S.sum(); if s>0 S/=s
   W=(1.0-smooth_alpha)*W + smooth_alpha*S
   s=W.sum(); if s>0 W/=s
return W
```
If K small=2, gap floor could be large relative? fine.

`local_descent` detailed pseudocode with variables:
```
def local_descent(a0, n, mode, budget, deadline, rng, k_min_frac=0.02, k_max_frac=0.5, smooth_start=0.5, smooth_end=0.1, eta_start=0.25, label="", no_improve_limit=45.0):
    info = make_info(n)? To avoid recompute, pass info. Let's pass info.
    if mode == 'sym':
        b = a_to_b_sym(a0,n)
        cur_var = b
    else:
        cur_var = _safe_norm(a0)
    # Build cur_a
    if mode == 'sym': cur_a = build_a_sym(cur_var,n); cur_a = _safe_norm(cur_a)
    else: cur_a = cur_var.copy()
    L,Nscore,Ngrad,idx = info
    cur_M = max_M(cur_a,L,Nscore)
    best_M = cur_M; best_a = cur_a.copy(); best_var = cur_var.copy()
    eta = eta_start
    fail=0
    t0=time.monotonic(); last_improve=t0
    n_iter=0
    it_since_sym=0
    while time.monotonic() < min(deadline, t0+budget):
       t=time.monotonic()
       elapsed=t-t0; progress=min(1.0, elapsed/budget)
       K = int(min(L, max(64, L*(k_min_frac + (k_max_frac-k_min_frac)*(progress**0.6)))))
       smooth_alpha = smooth_start + (smooth_end-smooth_start)*progress
       C,F = compute_C_F(cur_a,Ngrad,L)
       M=float(C.max())
       # maybe if M < best_M: update
       if M < best_M - 1e-15:
           best_M=M; best_a=cur_a.copy(); if mode sym best_var=cur_var.copy() else; last_improve=t
       elif M < cur_M:
           cur_M=M # shouldn't
       it_since_sym+=1
       if it_since_sym >= 500:
          it_since_sym=0
          a_sym=0.5*(cur_a+cur_a[::-1]); a_sym=_safe_norm(a_sym)
          Ms=max_M(a_sym,L,Nscore)
          if Ms < best_M - 1e-15:
             best_M=Ms; best_a=a_sym.copy(); last_improve=t
             if mode == 'sym': best_var=a_to_b_sym(best_a,n)
             else: best_var=best_a.copy()
          if Ms < cur_M - 1e-15:
             cur_M=Ms; cur_a=a_sym.copy()
             if mode == 'sym': cur_var=a_to_b_sym(cur_a,n)
             else: cur_var=cur_a.copy()
       W=make_weights(C,M,K,smooth_alpha)
       Ga=gradient_from_W(W,F,Ngrad,idx)
       if mode == 'sym': Gb=grad_a_to_b(Ga,n)
       else: Gb=Ga.copy()
       gd=Gb - np.mean(Gb)
       span=gd.max()-gd.min()
       if not np.isfinite(span) or span<=1e-30:
          # perturb
       else:
          accepted=False
          for e in (eta, 0.5*eta, 0.25*eta):
              if e <= 0.005: break? Maybe min 0.01
              if mode sym:
                 b_try=mirror_sym(cur_var,gd,span,e)
                 a_try=build_a_sym(b_try,n); a_try=_safe_norm(a_try)
              else:
                 a_try=mirror_full(cur_var,gd,span,e)
              M_try=max_M(a_try,L,Nscore)
              if np.isfinite(M_try) and M_try < cur_M - 1e-14:
                  accepted=True
                  cur_var = ...; cur_a=a_try; cur_M=M_try; eta=e; fail=0
                  if M_try < best_M - 1e-15: update best; last_improve=t
                  break
          if not accepted:
              fail += 1
              eta = max(0.02, eta*0.85)
              if fail >= 8:
                  # perturb/reset
                  if rng.random()<0.6:
                     if mode sym: cur_var=perturb_var(cur_var, rng, 0.08)
                     else: cur_var=perturb_full(cur_var,rng,0.08)
                  else:
                     cur_var=best_var.copy()
                     if rng.random()<0.7:
                        if mode sym: cur_var=perturb_var(cur_var,rng,0.03)
                        else: cur_var=perturb_full(cur_var,rng,0.03)
                  build cur_a, cur_M
                  fail=0; eta=0.12
       n_iter+=1
       if t - last_improve > 2*no_improve_limit:
          # if start was poor maybe stop
          # But for main we might want continue until budget? Use early stop.
          break
       if t - last_improve > no_improve_limit and eta < 0.03:
          # Could perturb and reset eta
          ...
    return best_a,best_M
```

`make_features(n,d)`:
```
def make_features(n,d):
  x = (2*np.arange(n,float)/(n-1) -1).astype(float)
  cols=[]
  # constant
  c=np.ones(n); cols.append(c)
  if d>1:
    T0=np.ones(n); T1=x.copy()
    for k in range(2,d+1):
       T2=2*x*T1 - T0
       T0,T1=T1,T2
       c=T2.copy(); c -= c.mean(); norm=np.linalg.norm(c)
       if norm>1e-12:
          c = c * (math.sqrt(n)/norm)  # RMS 1
          cols.append(c)
  # For T1 also normalize
  if len(cols)>1:
     c1 = x.copy(); c1-=c1.mean(); norm=np.linalg.norm(c1); if norm>... c1*=sqrt(n)/norm; cols[1]=c1
  # For k>=2 normalized
  F=np.column_stack(cols)
  # optionally orthogonalize under uniform to improve conditioning:
  # Gram-Schmidt: for j in range(d): for i<j subtract dot/n? Since columns O1, dot O1. Normalize each to RMS1 after orth.
```
Better: Build raw Cheb columns normalized RMS1, then orthonormalize under uniform:
```
Q=[]
for c in raw:
   v=c.copy()
   for u in Q: v -= np.dot(u,v)/(n) * u? If u RMS1, projection coefficient = dot(u,v)/n.
   norm=np.linalg.norm(v)/sqrt(n)
   if norm>1e-6: v = v/(norm*sqrt(n)) # RMS1
   Q.append(v)
F=column_stack(Q)
```
This keeps RMS1, orthonormal under uniform. Constant column. Good.
Need D = number columns; may be less than d if linear dependent (no). Input d maybe number of nonconstant? Let's define `make_features(n, d)` returns d columns including constant? We can request total_d. Simpler: total_d = d; raw Cheb up to d-1. For d=8, columns T0..T7.
Implementation:
```
def make_features(n,total_d):
  x=...
  raws=[]
  T0=np.ones(n); raws.append(T0.copy())
  if total_d>1:
    T1=x.copy(); raws.append(T1.copy())
    for k in range(2,total_d):
       Tk=2*x*T1-T0; raws.append(Tk.copy()); T0,T1=T1,Tk
  cols=[]
  for c in raws:
    v=c.copy()
    # make mean zero for nonconstant? after orth contain constant, okay.
    for u in cols:
       v -= (np.dot(u,v)/n)*u
    norm=np.linalg.norm(v)
    if norm < 1e-9* n: continue
    v = v / (norm / math.sqrt(n))  # RMS 1
    cols.append(v)
  F=np.column_stack(cols)
  return F
```
Check normalization: If v RMS R = norm/sqrt(n), dividing by R gives RMS1: v/= (norm/√n). Yes.
Columns orthonormal under uniform: dot/ n =1.
Total_d maybe 20.

`optimize_params(a0,n,info,d,budget,deadline,rng,eta_start,global_best_score? not needed)`:
- F=make_features
- initial theta from a0 plus random perturbation? We can pass `theta_init` or random. For diverse, create several:
   - projected + random `rng.normal(0,0.2,d)`
   - projected + random `rng.normal(0,1.0,d)`
   - pure random `rng.normal(0,1.0,d)`
- local variables.
- t0; best_m, best_a.
- lr = 10000? Need decide. Since F RMS1, gradθ ~? We need line search adapt. Let's estimate more: a~1/n. G from active weights: We used no factor 2, gradient of weighted sum W·C? W sum1; G_j≈∑W a. If peaks active, G maybe O(1/n). a*G ~1/n^2=2.7e-8. F entries O1. B_ag sum ~0.01? Actually n terms *2.7e-8 =0.00016. Sumag same. gradθ ~1e-4. lr=1000 gives θ change 0.1; φ change 0.1, multiplicative exp 0.1. Good. Start lr=2000. If no improve, reduce. Step may need 10000 to make big change. Could normalize gradient: direction=gradθ; normg; `theta_try = theta - step * gradθ / normg` where step is θ magnitude. Then choose step initial 5.0 (φ L2 change 5, significant). But if gradient direction may not be steepest in Euclidean θ? It is negative gradient; line search along unit direction. Need step magnitude. Parameter magnitudes tens; 5 moderate. Let's use normalized direction and `step_start=5.0`, adjust. Because if gradθ tiny, normalized direction still meaningful. But if gradient noise, step 5 maybe large. Use line search [step, step/2, step/4]. If fail, step*=0.7. This is scale-free. Good.
- Use same K/weights schedule. Compute `G` from active weights. `grad_theta = F.T@(a*G) - sumag*(F.T@a)`. `norm=np.linalg.norm(grad_theta)`. If norm small break.
- direction=grad_theta/norm. For steps in `steps=(step, step*0.5, step*0.25)`, `theta_try=theta - s*direction`. Evaluate M. Accept best if <cur_M. If none, fail; step*=0.8; perturb theta if fail.

Pair polish implementation in detail:
```
def pair_polish(a0, info, deadline, rng, passes=100, top_select=16, fracs=(0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,1.0)):
    a=_safe_norm(a0); L,Nscore,Ngrad,idx=info; n=len(a)
    C,F=compute_C_F(a,Ngrad,L); M=C.max()
    best_M=M; best_a=a.copy()
    for pass in range(passes):
       if time>=deadline: break
       # update C,F,M from current? We maintain exact C maybe.
       # make weights for selection:
       K=max(64, L//8)
       W=make_weights(C,M,K,0.2)
       F=np.fft.rfft(a,Ngrad) # need current F; we have if C from compute. But after updates, recompute? We'll recompute each pass.
       G=gradient_from_W(W,F,Ngrad,idx)
       # donor/receiver indices
       d_idx=np.argpartition(G, -top_select)[-top_select:]
       r_idx=np.argpartition(G, top_select)
       rand_idx=rng.integers(0,n, size=2*top_select)
       # combine unique
       donors=list(set(d_idx.tolist()+rand_idx.tolist())) maybe firsts
       receivers=...
       improved=False
       for i in donors[:8]:
         for j in receivers[:8]:
            if i==j: continue
            ai=a[i]; aj=a[j]; if ai+aj<eps: continue
            lin=np.zeros(L)
            lin[j:j+n] += 2.0*a
            lin[i:i+n] -= 2.0*a
            best_val=M; best_delta=0
            for frac in fracs:
               d1=frac*ai
               if d1>1e-18:
                  val=eval_pair(C,lin,d1,2*i,2*j,i+j)
                  if val<best_val: best_val=val; best_delta=d1
               d2=-frac*aj
               if d2<-1e-18:
                  val=eval_pair(C,lin,d2,2*i,2*j,i+j)
                  if val<best_val: best_val=val; best_delta=d2
            if best_val < M - 1e-13:
               # apply
               a[i]-=best_delta; a[j]+=best_delta
               a=np.maximum(a,0); # sum maybe unchanged, normalize
               s=a.sum(); if s>0: a/=s
               C += best_delta*lin
               dq=best_delta*best_delta
               C[2*i] += dq; C[2*j] += dq; C[i+j] -= 2*dq
               C=np.maximum(C,0)
               # renormalize C? sum may drift tiny; scale= C.sum(); if close to1: C/=C.sum()? But M would scale? Since score uses M (sum fixed). If we normalize a, C from unnormalized? We applied exact update for sum unchanged a; but then we renormalize a slightly if sum drift. To keep C consistent, recompute C via FFT after each accept? Maybe not.
               # Better: Don't normalize a after pair if sum should remain 1. But floating sum may 1+1e-15. We can adjust C by sum factor? Simpler recompute C,M from a via FFT after accept to correct.
               # Recompute costly but saccepts maybe few. Do it.
               C,F=compute_C_F(a,Ngrad,L); M=float(C.max())
               if M<best_M: best_M=M; best_a=a.copy()
               improved=True
               # break? continue to try more pairs this pass
        if not improved and pass>10:
            # small perturbation?
            # maybe break
            break
    return best_a,best_M
```
`eval_pair`:
```
def eval_pair(C,lin,d,ii,jj,kij):
   # avoid allocate? We'll allocate.
   tmp = C + d*lin
   dq=d*d
   tmp[ii] += dq
   tmp[jj] += dq
   tmp[kij] -= 2*dq
   # Note if positions overlap, sequential additions/differences ok.
   return float(tmp.max())
```

Implement `optimize_params`:
```
def optimize_params(a0,n,info,F,theta,budget,deadline,rng,label=''):
    L,Nscore,Ngrad,idx=info
    a=param_to_a(theta,F); M=max_M(a,L,Nscore)
    best=a.copy(); best_M=M; cur_theta=theta.copy(); cur_a=a; cur_M=M
    step=5.0; fail=0; t0=...; last=...
    while time < min(deadline,t0+budget):
       progress...
       K...
       C,Ff=compute_C_F(cur_a,Ngrad,L)
       M=C.max()
       if M<best_M: update
       W=make_weights(C,M,K, 0.5-0.4*progress)
       Ga=gradient_from_W(W,Ff,Ngrad,idx)
       ag=cur_a*Ga
       sumag=ag.sum()
       ma=F.T @ cur_a
       bg=F.T @ ag
       grad_theta=bg - sumag*ma
       norm=np.linalg.norm(grad_theta)
       if norm<1e-14: break
       direction=grad_theta/norm
       accepted=False
       for s in (step, step*0.5, step*0.25):
          th=cur_theta - s*direction
          a_try=param_to_a(th,F)
          M_try=max_M(a_try,L,Nscore)
          if M_try < cur_M - 1e-12:
             accept; cur_theta=th; cur_a=a_try; cur_M=M_try; step=s; fail=0; if M_try<best...
             break
       if not accepted:
          fail+=1; step=max(0.05, step*0.8)
          if fail>=8:
             # random theta perturbation or reset to best
             if rng.random()<0.5:
                cur_theta = cur_theta + rng.normal(0, 0.5, F.shape[1])
             else:
                cur_theta = best_theta.copy()
                cur_theta = cur_theta + rng.normal(0,0.2,...)
             cur_a=param_to_a(...); cur_M=...
             step=3.0; fail=0
       if time-last > 90: break
    return best_a,best_M,best_theta
```
Need store best_theta. ok.
`F.T @ cur_a` with F shape n x d. Good.
Step magnitude: Since direction unit in θ, step 5 changes φ L2 by 5. Good. Maybe start 3. For initial random, step 3. If fails, reduce.

But if gradient direction from weighted c is not aligned with max due parameterization, step 5 may overshoot; line search halves.

Pair polish: We need `eval_pair` efficient. Let's write top-level.

```
def pair_eval(C,lin,d,ii,jj,kij):
    # base = C + d*lin
    # To avoid allocating 3 times per candidate? Accept.
    tmp = C + d*lin
    dq=d*d
    if ii < len(tmp): tmp[ii]+=dq
    if jj < len(tmp): tmp[jj]+=dq
    if kij < len(tmp): tmp[kij]-=2*dq
    return float(tmp.max())
```
Indices always valid: ii=2i up to 2n-2 L-1; jj=2j; kij=i+j up to 2n-2. Good.
When applying accepted pair, if we recompute C via FFT, no need update. But recompute after each acceptance might be many. Acceptances maybe dozens. `compute_C_F` with Ngrad; okay.

`_safe_norm`:
```
def _safe_norm(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        x = np.ones_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(x.sum())
    return x / s
```
Need import math.

`make_info`:
```
def make_info(n):
    L = 2*n - 1
    Nscore = 1 << max(1, (L-1).bit_length())
    size = 3*n - 2
    Ngrad = 1 << max(1, (size-1).bit_length())
    idx = 2*n - 2 - np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    return L, Nscore, Ngrad, idx
```
For n=1, L=1, Nscore=(0).bit_length? max1 ->1<<1=2? Actually size=1, Nscore=1<<max(1,(0).bit_length())=2; Ngrad size1 ->2. Good. But n likely large.

`compute_C_F`:
```
def compute_C_F(a, N, L):
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    C = np.fft.irfft(F * F, N)[:L]
    C = 0.5 * (C + C[::-1])
    C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)
    return C, F
```
If C sum maybe not 1 due scaling? a sum1; okay.

`max_M`:
```
def max_M(a, L, Nscore):
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, Nscore)
    C = np.fft.irfft(F*F, Nscore)[:L]
    return float(np.max(C))
```
Could sym/clip not needed for max. But as numeric; maybe `C = np.maximum(C,0)`. no.

`make_weights` needs `K` maybe np int. Avoid division by zero.
```
def make_weights(C, M, K, smooth_alpha):
    L = C.size
    if K <= 1:
       idx_active = np.array([int(np.argmax(C))])
    elif K >= L:
       idx_active = np.arange(L)
    else:
       idx_active = np.argpartition(C, -K)[-K:]
    gaps = M - C[idx_active]
    floor = max(1e-15, 1e-8 * max(1.0, float(M)))
    W = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    W[idx_active] = 1.0 / (gaps + floor)
    s = W.sum()
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
       W[int(np.argmax(C))]=1.0
       s=1.0
    W /= s
    W = 0.5*(W + W[::-1])
    s = W.sum()
    if s > 0 and math.isfinite(s): W/=s
    if smooth_alpha > 0:
       S = C.copy()
       sS=S.sum()
       if sS>0 and math.isfinite(sS): S/=sS
       else: S[:]=0; S[L//2]=1
       S=0.5*(S+S[::-1]); sS=S.sum(); if sS>0 S/=sS
       W = (1.0 - smooth_alpha)*W + smooth_alpha*S
       sW=W.sum(); if sW>0 and math.isfinite(sW): W/=sW
    return W
```
If M small 1e-4, floor 1e-12? `max(1e-15,1e-8*M)`=1e-12 if M=1e-4? Actually 1e-8*1e-4=1e-12. Good. If M large 0.1 floor 1e-9.

`gradient_from_W`:
```
def gradient_from_W(W, F, N, idx):
    Wr = W[::-1]
    G = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(Wr, N) * F, N)
    return G[idx].copy()
```
Should we multiply by `2`? Direction same. Step size tuned perhaps assuming no 2; factor 2 just scale gradient, but we scale by span, so factor cancels. Good.

`mirror_step_variable(var, gd_centered, span, eta)`:
```
def mirror_step_variable(var, gd, span, eta):
    logv = np.log(var + 1e-300) - eta * gd / span
    logv -= logv.max()
    nv = np.exp(logv)
    return _safe_norm(nv)
```
Where gd already centered. If span small handled outside.

For odd n=5 m=3: `ga[4:2:-1]` -> [4,3] length2. good.

```
def a_to_b_sym(a,n):
```

Perturb functions:
```
def perturb_variable(var, rng, strength=0.1):
    m=len(var)
    v=var.copy()
    mode=int(rng.integers(0,4))
    if m > 1:
       t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
    else: t=np.zeros(1)
    if mode==0:
       al=rng.uniform(-1.0,1.0)
       v *= np.exp(al*(t-0.5))
    elif mode==1:
       freq=int(rng.integers(1,5))
       ph=rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*math.pi)
       amp=rng.uniform(0.05, 0.35)
       v *= (1.0 + amp*np.sin(2.0*math.pi*freq*t + ph))
    elif mode==2:
       i=int(rng.integers(0,m)); j=int(rng.integers(0,m))
       if i != j:
          amt = var[i]*rng.uniform(0.05,0.3)
          v[i] -= amt; v[j] += amt
    else:
       freq=int(rng.integers(1,3)); ph1... add two sines rng.uniform(0,1)
    return _safe_norm(v)
```
Works for full a or sym b.

`local_descent` code exact:
```
def local_descent(a0,n,info,mode,budget,deadline,rng, label='', kmin=0.02,kmax=0.35, smooth0=0.5,smooth1=0.05, eta0=0.25, no_improve=45.0):
    L,Nscore,Ngrad,idx=info
    if mode == 'sym':
       var=a_to_b_sym(a0,n)
    else:
       var=_safe_norm(a0)
    if mode=='sym':
       cur_a=build_a_sym(var,n); cur_a=_safe_norm(cur_a)
    else:
       cur_a=var.copy()
    cur_M=max_M(cur_a,L,Nscore)
    best_M=cur_M; best_a=cur_a.copy(); best_var=var.copy(); best_theta? no
    eta=eta0; fail=0; t0=time.monotonic(); last_improve=t0; it=0; sym_count=0
    budget=max(5.0,budget)
    end=min(deadline,t0+budget)
    while time.monotonic() < end:
       t=time.monotonic()
       if t >= end: break
       elapsed=t-t0
       progress=min(1.0, elapsed/budget)
       K=int(min(L, max(64, L*(kmin + (kmax-kmin)*(progress**0.6)))))
       smooth=smooth0 + (smooth1-smooth0)*progress
       C,F=compute_C_F(cur_a,Ngrad,L)
       M=float(C.max())
       if M < best_M - 1e-15:
          best_M=M; best_a=cur_a.copy(); best_var=var.copy(); last_improve=t
       elif M < cur_M:
          cur_M=M
       # sym every 400 iters or 5 sec?
       sym_count += 1
       if sym_count >= 400 or t-last_improve > 10:
          sym_count=0
          a_sym=0.5*(cur_a+cur_a[::-1]); a_sym=_safe_norm(a_sym)
          Ms=max_M(a_sym,L,Nscore)
          if Ms < best_M - 1e-15:
             best_M=Ms; best_a=a_sym.copy(); if mode=='sym': best_var=a_to_b_sym(a_sym,n) else best_var=a_sym.copy(); last_improve=t
          if Ms < cur_M - 1e-15:
             cur_M=Ms; cur_a=a_sym.copy()
             if mode=='sym': var=a_to_b_sym(cur_a,n)
             else: var=cur_a.copy()
       W=make_weights(C,M,K,smooth)
       Ga=gradient_from_W(W,F,Ngrad,idx)
       if mode == 'sym': Gb=grad_a_to_b(Ga,n)
       else: Gb=Ga
       gd=Gb - float(np.mean(Gb))
       span=float(gd.max() - gd.min())
       if not math.isfinite(span) or span <= 1e-30:
          # skip/perturb
          fail +=1
       else:
          accepted=False
          # eta candidates
          e1=eta; e2=0.5*eta; e3=0.25*eta
          for e in (e1,e2,e3):
             if e <= 0.005: break
             if mode == 'sym':
                vtry=mirror_step_variable(var,gd,span,e)
                at=build_a_sym(vtry,n); at=_safe_norm(at)
             else:
                at=mirror_step_variable(var,gd,span,e)
                # var same? mirror returns normalized
             Mtry=max_M(at,L,Nscore)
             if math.isfinite(Mtry) and Mtry < cur_M - 1e-12:
                accepted=True
                if mode == 'sym': var=vtry
                else: var=at
                cur_a=at; cur_M=Mtry; eta=e; fail=0
                if Mtry < best_M - 1e-15:
                   best_M=Mtry; best_a=at.copy(); if mode=='sym': best_var=var.copy() else best_var=at.copy(); last_improve=t
                break
          if not accepted:
             fail += 1
             if fail >= 8:
                # perturb/reset
                if rng.random() < 0.65:
                   var = perturb_variable(var, rng, 0.1)
                else:
                   var = best_var.copy()
                   var = perturb_variable(var, rng, 0.03)
                if mode == 'sym':
                   cur_a=build_a_sym(var,n); cur_a=_safe_norm(cur_a)
                else:
                   cur_a=var.copy()
                cur_M=max_M(cur_a,L,Nscore)
                fail=0; eta=0.12
             else:
                eta = max(0.02, eta * 0.85)
       it +=1
       if t - last_improve > 2*no_improve:
          break
    # final sym check
    a_sym=...; if better update best
    return best_a,best_M
```

`make_features`: Need handle total_d=1 (constant only). But if constant only, phi constant, a uniform. Gradient zero. We'll use d>=8.
Code:
```
def make_features(n,total_d):
    x = (2.0*np.arange(n, dtype=float)/max(1,n-1) - 1.0)
    raws=[]
    T0 = np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    raws.append(T0.copy())
    if total_d > 1:
        T1 = x.copy()
        raws.append(T1.copy())
        for k in range(2,total_d):
            T2 = 2.0*x*T1 - T0
            raws.append(T2.copy())
            T0, T1 = T1, T2
    cols=[]
    for c in raws:
        v=c.astype(float).copy()
        for u in cols:
            coeff = float(np.dot(u, v) / n)
            v -= coeff * u
        norm = float(np.linalg.norm(v))
        if norm <= 1e-12 * n:
            continue
        v = v / (norm / math.sqrt(n))
        cols.append(v)
    if not cols: cols.append(np.ones(n))
    F=np.column_stack(cols)
    return F
```
For n=6000, Tort total_d 20, fine. Cheb T1 may be nearly orthogonal to constant; after orth constant removed? We orthonormalize, good.

```
def optimize_params(a0,n,info,F,theta0,budget,deadline,rng,label=''):
    L,Nscore,Ngrad,idx=info
    theta=np.asarray(theta0,float).copy()
    a=param_to_a(theta,F); M=max_M(a,...)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_M=M; best_theta=theta.copy()
    cur_a=a; cur_M=M; cur_theta=theta
    step=3.0; fail=0; t0...; last=t0; end=min(deadline,t0+budget)
    d=F.shape[1]
    while time<end:
      progress...
      K...
      C,Ff=compute_C_F(...)
      M=C.max()
      if M<best_M: update (best_theta=cur_theta.copy())
      W=...
      Ga=...
      ag=cur_a*Ga
      sumag=float(ag.sum())
      ma=F.T @ cur_a
      bg=F.T @ ag
      grad_theta=bg - sumag*ma
      normg=float(np.linalg.norm(grad_theta))
      if not finite or normg < 1e-14: break
      direction=grad_theta/normg
      accepted=False
      for s in (step, 0.5*step, 0.25*step):
         th=cur_theta - s*direction
         at=param_to_a(th,F)
         Mt=max_M(at,...)
         if Mt < cur_M - 1e-12:
            accept; step=s; fail=0; if Mt<best...
            break
      if not accepted:
        fail +=1
        step=max(0.05, step*0.8)
        if fail >= 8:
           if rng.random()<0.5:
              cur_theta = cur_theta + rng.normal(0.0,0.4,d)
           else:
              cur_theta = best_theta.copy()
              cur_theta = cur_theta + rng.normal(0.0,0.2,d)
           cur_a=param_to_a(cur_theta,F); cur_M=max_M(...)
           step=2.0; fail=0
      if t-last > 60: break
    return best_a,best_M,best_theta
```

```
def pair_polish(a0, info, deadline, rng, passes=200, top_select=10):
    L,Nscore,Ngrad,idx=info; n=len(a0)
    a=_safe_norm(a0)
    C,F=compute_C_F(a,Ngrad,L); M=float(C.max())
    best_a=a.copy(); best_M=M
    no_imp=0
    for p in range(passes):
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       K=max(64, L//8)
       W=make_weights(C,M,K,0.15)
       G=gradient_from_W(W,F,Ngrad,idx)
       # indices
       d_idx=np.argpartition(G, -top_select)[-top_select:]
       r_idx=np.argpartition(G, top_select)
       # add random
       rand=rng.integers(0,n,size=top_select)
       donor_list=list(np.unique(np.concatenate((d_idx,rand))))
       recv_list=list(np.unique(np.concatenate((r_idx,rand))))
       # limit length to top_select+2
       donor_list=donor_list[:top_select+2]
       recv_list=...
       improved=False
       for i in donor_list:
          i=int(i)
          if a[i] <= 0: continue? still can receive.
          for j in recv_list:
             j=int(j)
             if i==j: continue
             ai=a[i]; aj=a[j]
             if ai + aj <= 1e-18: continue
             lin=np.zeros(L)
             lin[j:j+n] += 2.0*a
             lin[i:i+n] -= 2.0*a
             ii=2*i; jj=2*j; kij=i+j
             best_val=M; best_delta=0.0
             for frac in (0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,1.0):
                 d=frac*ai
                 if d>1e-18:
                    val=pair_eval(C,lin,d,ii,jj,kij)
                    if val < best_val: best_val=val; best_delta=d
                 d=-frac*aj
                 if d < -1e-18:
                    val=pair_eval(C,lin,d,ii,jj,kij)
                    if val < best_val: best_val=val; best_delta=d
             if best_val < M - 1e-13 and best_delta != 0.0:
                 a[i] -= best_delta; a[j] += best_delta
                 a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
                 s=a.sum()
                 if s>0: a/=s
                 C,F=compute_C_F(a,Ngrad,L)
                 M=float(C.max())
                 improved=True
                 if M < best_M:
                    best_M=M; best_a=a.copy()
       if improved: no_imp=0
       else:
          no_imp +=1
          if no_imp >= 10: break
    return best_a,best_M
```

```
def spectral_constmag_start(n,rng,iters=150,beta=0.6, sym=True):
    # initial random smooth
    if sym:
       a = random_smooth_sequence(n,rng)
       a = 0.5*(a+a[::-1]); a=_safe_norm(a)
    N = 1 << (4*n-1).bit_length()  # maybe 32768/65536
    for _ in range(iters):
       F=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
       mag=np.abs(F); mag_safe=np.maximum(mag,1e-300)
       # target mag: power compress
       # use mean over non-DC
       mu = float(np.mean(mag[1:])) if n>1 else 1.0
       target = np.power(mag_safe, beta) * (mu ** (1.0-beta))
       F2 = F * (target / mag_safe)
       new = np.fft.irfft(F2,N)[:n]
       if sym: new=0.5*(new+new[::-1])
       new=np.maximum(new,0)
       s=new.sum()
       if s < 0.5*a.sum(): break
       a=new/s
    return a
```
N maybe for n=6121: 4n=24484 -> (24483).bit_length=15? 2^14=16384, 2^15=32768. Good. Iters 100; 100*FFT 32768 ~0.05s each? 5s per start. Use maybe 4 starts beta [0.3,0.5,0.7,0.9], sym true. Add to starts. This is cheap and different. But target mag compress may keep DC same? It changes DC magnitude? It uses all mag including DC; target for DC = mag0^beta * mu^{1-beta}, which may change DC drastically, and inverse changes sum. We normalize after clip. Maybe okay. To preserve sum, could set target[0]=mag[0] (DC unchanged), and non-DC target=... Because DC controls sum; normalizing later anyway, but changing DC affects shape? DC is total, inverse scaling. Better set target[0]=mag[0] to avoid changing overall scale, and compress non-DC. Then F2 DC same. Use:
```
target = mag_safe.copy()
non = np.ones(N//2+1)
mu=np.mean(mag[1:])
target[1:] = (mag_safe[1:]**beta)*(mu**(1-beta))
```

Random smooth sequence function:
```
def random_smooth_sequence(n,rng):
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    y=np.ones(n)
    for _ in range(3):
       f=int(rng.integers(1,6))
       ph=rng.uniform(0,2π)
       amp=rng.uniform(0.1,0.5)
       y += amp*np.sin(2π*f*x+ph)
    y=np.maximum(y,0.05)
    return _safe_norm(y)
```
Good.

Detailed code snippets:

```
def pair_eval(C, lin, d, ii, jj, kij):
    tmp = C + d * lin
    dq = d * d
    tmp[ii] += dq
    tmp[jj] += dq
    tmp[kij] -= 2.0 * dq
    return float(np.max(tmp))
```
If `ii, jj, kij` valid. Good.

`mirror_step_variable` code:
```
def mirror_step_variable(var, gd, span, eta):
    if span <= 1e-30:
        return var.copy()
    logv = np.log(var + 1e-300) - eta * gd / span
    logv -= logv.max()
    logv = np.nan_to_num(logv, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=-1000.0)
    nv = np.exp(logv)
    return _safe_norm(nv)
```
If logv max -inf? var zeros all? var normalized not all zero. logv max finite.

```python
"""
Nonnegative step-function optimizer for
    score(a) = 2 * len(a) * max(convolution(a, a)) / (sum(a))^2.

This program专 combines several complementary searches:

1. Differentiable active-peak mirror descent.
   - The objective is normalized to sum(a)=1, so we minimize max(a*a).
   - A dense subgradient of a smoothed max is formed from the heaviest
     convolution lags using inverse-gap weights, plus a mild c-weighted
     smoothing term.
   - The descent map is the multiplicative / mirror update on the simplex.
   - Both an unconstrained full-vector mode and a symmetric half-vector mode
     are used.

2. Low-dimensional strictly-nonnegative parametric searches.
   - a_i = softmax(F @ theta), where F is a Chebyshev feature matrix.
   - This finds smooth basins that dense full-dimensional local moves can
     then refine.

3. Frequency-domain nonnegative projection starts.
   - Flattens non-DC magnitude estimates and projects back to nonnegative,
     symmetric time-domain sequences.

4. Exact pair-transfer polish.
   - Moves mass between two coordinates and evaluates the exact quadratic
     change of the whole convolution to shave the final peak.

The global best sequence is returned.
"""

import math
import time
import numpy as np


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Basic helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _safe_norm(x):
    """Clip to nonnegative and normalize to sum 1."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        x = np.ones_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(x.sum())
    return x / s


def make_info(n):
    """FFT sizes and index array needed by the fast convolution routines."""
    L = 2 * n - 1
    Nscore = 1 << max(1, (L - 1).bit_length())
    grad_len = 3 * n - 2
    Ngrad = 1 << max(1, (grad_len - 1).bit_length())
    idx = 2 * n - 2 - np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    return L, Nscore, Ngrad, idx


def compute_C_F(a, N, L):
    """Full linear convolution a*a and its FFT (zero-padded to N)."""
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    C = np.fft.irfft(F * F, N)[:L]
    C = 0.5 * (C + C[::-1])
    C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)
    return C, F


def max_M(a, L, Nscore):
    """Fast max coefficient of a*a, assuming len(a) is fixed."""
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, Nscore)
    C = np.fft.irfft(F * F, Nscore)[:L]
    return float(np.max(C))


def start_sort_key(item):
    return item[0]


def add_start(starts, name, a, info):
    a = _safe_norm(a)
    L, Nscore, Ngrad, idx = info
    M = max_M(a, L, Nscore)
    starts.append((M, name, a))
    return M


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Weighted subgradient of the max-conv coefficient
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def make_weights(C, M, K, smooth_alpha):
    """
    Weights over convolution lags.

    - Inverse-gap weights on the top K lags emphasize active peaks.
    - Mixing with C penalizes broad variance and prevents getting stuck
      when only one lag is active.
    """
    L = C.size
    if K <= 1:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(C))], dtype=np.int64)
    elif K >= L:
        idx = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int64)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(C, -K)[-K:]

    gaps = M - C[idx]
    floor = max(1e-15, 1e-8 * max(1.0, float(M)))
    W = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    W[idx] = 1.0 / (gaps + floor)

    s = float(W.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        W[:] = 0.0
        W[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0
        s = 1.0
    W /= s

    # Convolution coefficients are symmetric; enforce symmetric weights.
    W = 0.5 * (W + W[::-1])
    s = float(W.sum())
    if math.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
        W /= s

    if smooth_alpha > 0.0:
        S = C.copy()
        sS = float(S.sum())
        if math.isfinite(sS) and sS > 0.0:
            S /= sS
        else:
            S[:] = 0.0
            S[L // 2] = 1.0
        S = 0.5 * (S + S[::-1])
        sS = float(S.sum())
        if math.isfinite(sS) and sS > 0.0:
            S /= sS
        W = (1.0 - smooth_alpha) * W + smooth_alpha * S
        sW = float(W.sum())
        if math.isfinite(sW) and sW > 0.0:
            W /= sW

    return W


def gradient_from_W(W, F, N, idx):
    """
    Gradient (up to an irrelevant positive scale) of sum_k W_k * conv(a,a)_k
    with respect to a.  Returns length n vector.
    """
    Wr = W[::-1]
    G = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(Wr, N) * F, N)
    return G[idx].copy()


def mirror_step_variable(var, gd_centered, span, eta):
    """Exponentiated step: var_i <- var_i * exp(-eta * gd_i / span)."""
    if span <= 1e-30:
        return var.copy()
    logv = np.log(var + 1e-300) - eta * gd_centered / span
    logv -= logv.max()
    logv = np.nan_to_num(logv, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=-1000.0)
    nv = np.exp(logv)
    return _safe_norm(nv)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Symmetric parameterization
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def build_a_sym(b, n):
    """Build the length-n symmetric sequence from its first half."""
    m = len(b)
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n % 2 == 1:
        a[:m] = b
        a[m:] = b[-2::-1]
    else:
        a[:m] = b
        a[m:] = b[::-1]
    return a


def a_to_b_sym(a, n):
    """Symmetrize a and return its independent first half."""
    a = 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])
    a = _safe_norm(a)
    if n % 2 == 1:
        b = a[: (n + 1) // 2]
    else:
        b = a[: n // 2]
    return _safe_norm(b)


def grad_a_to_b(ga, n):
    """Push a full length-n gradient down to the symmetric half-vector."""
    if n % 2 == 0:
        m = n // 2
        return ga[:m] + ga[n - 1:m - 1:-1]
    else:
        m = (n + 1) // 2
        gb = ga[:m].copy()
        if m > 1:
            gb[:-1] += ga[n - 1:m - 1:-1]
        return gb


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Perturbations
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def perturb_variable(var, rng, strength=0.1):
    """Smooth random perturbation in log/simplex space."""
    m = len(var)
    v = var.copy()
    if m <= 1:
        return _safe_norm(v)

    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 4))
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)

    if mode == 0:
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0))
        v *= np.exp(alpha * (t - 0.5))
    elif mode == 1:
        freq = int(rng.integers(1, 5))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.35))
        v *= (1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * freq * t + phase))
    elif mode == 2:
        i = int(rng.integers(0, m))
        j = int(rng.integers(0, m))
        if i != j:
            amt = var[i] * float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.30))
            v[i] -= amt
            v[j] += amt
    else:
        amp1 = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.30))
        amp2 = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.20))
        ph1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        ph2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        v *= (1.0 + amp1 * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * 1.5 * t + ph1)
                  + amp2 * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * 3.0 * t + ph2))

    return _safe_norm(v)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Full / symmetric local mirror descent
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def local_descent(a0, n, info, mode, budget, deadline, rng,
                  label="", kmin=0.02, kmax=0.35,
                  smooth0=0.5, smooth1=0.05, eta0=0.35,
                  no_improve_limit=45.0):
    """
    Local mirror descent.
    mode = 'full' optimizes all n coordinates.
    mode = 'sym' optimizes only the independent symmetric half.
    """
    L, Nscore, Ngrad, idx = info
    budget = max(5.0, float(budget))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    end = min(float(deadline), t0 + budget)

    if mode == "sym":
        var = a_to_b_sym(a0, n)
    else:
        var = _safe_norm(a0)

    if mode == "sym":
        cur_a = build_a_sym(var, n)
        cur_a = _safe_norm(cur_a)
    else:
        cur_a = var.copy()

    cur_M = max_M(cur_a, L, Nscore)
    best_M = cur_M
    best_a = cur_a.copy()
    best_var = var.copy()

    eta = float(eta0)
    fail = 0
    last_improve = t0
    last_print = t0
    sym_count = 0

    while time.monotonic() < end:
        t = time.monotonic()
        if t >= end:
            break

        elapsed = t - t0
        progress = min(1.0, elapsed / budget)

        K = int(min(L, max(64, L * (kmin + (kmax - kmin) * (progress ** 0.6)))))
        smooth_alpha = smooth0 + (smooth1 - smooth0) * progress

        C, F = compute_C_F(cur_a, Ngrad, L)
        M = float(C.max())

        if M < best_M - 1e-15:
            best_M = M
            best_a = cur_a.copy()
            best_var = var.copy()
            last_improve = t
            if t - last_print > 5.0:
                print(f"    {label}: local best M={M:.10f} score={2.0 * n * M:.7f}")
                last_print = t
        elif M < cur_M:
            cur_M = M

        sym_count += 1
        if sym_count >= 400 or t - last_improve > 10.0:
            sym_count = 0
            a_sym = 0.5 * (cur_a + cur_a[::-1])
            a_sym = _safe_norm(a_sym)
            Ms = max_M(a_sym, L, Nscore)
            if Ms < best_M - 1e-15:
                best_M = Ms
                best_a = a_sym.copy()
                if mode == "sym":
                    best_var = a_to_b_sym(a_sym, n)
                else:
                    best_var = a_sym.copy()
                last_improve = t
            if Ms < cur_M - 1e-15:
                cur_M = Ms
                cur_a = a_sym.copy()
                if mode == "sym":
                    var = a_to_b_sym(cur_a, n)
                else:
                    var = cur_a.copy()

        W = make_weights(C, M, K, smooth_alpha)
        Ga = gradient_from_W(W, F, Ngrad, idx)
        if mode == "sym":
            Gb = grad_a_to_b(Ga, n)
        else:
            Gb = Ga

        gd = Gb - float(np.mean(Gb))
        span = float(gd.max() - gd.min())

        if not math.isfinite(span) or span <= 1e-30:
            fail += 1
        else:
            accepted = False
            step_candidates = (eta, 0.5 * eta, 0.25 * eta)

            for e in step_candidates:
                if e <= 0.005:
                    break

                if mode == "sym":
                    vtry = mirror_step_variable(var, gd, span, e)
                    at = build_a_sym(vtry, n)
                    at = _safe_norm(at)
                else:
                    at = mirror_step_variable(var, gd, span, e)

                Mtry = max_M(at, L, Nscore)
                if math.isfinite(Mtry) and Mtry < cur_M - 1e-12:
                    accepted = True
                    if mode == "sym":
                        var = vtry
                    else:
                        var = at
                    cur_a = at
                    cur_M = Mtry
                    fail = 0
                    if e == step_candidates[0]:
                        eta = min(0.60, eta * 1.03)
                    else:
                        eta = float(e)

                    if Mtry < best_M - 1e-15:
                        best_M = Mtry
                        best_a = at.copy()
                        best_var = var.copy()
                        last_improve = t
                        if t - last_print > 5.0:
                            print(f"    {label}: local best M={Mtry:.10f} score={2.0 * n * Mtry:.7f}")
                            last_print = t
                    break

            if not accepted:
                fail += 1
                if fail >= 8:
                    if rng.random() < 0.65:
                        var = perturb_variable(var, rng, 0.10)
                    else:
                        var = best_var.copy()
                        var = perturb_variable(var, rng, 0.03)

                    if mode == "sym":
                        cur_a = build_a_sym(var, n)
                        cur_a = _safe_norm(cur_a)
                    else:
                        cur_a = var.copy()
                    cur_M = max_M(cur_a, L, Nscore)
                    fail = 0
                    eta = 0.12
                else:
                    eta = max(0.02, eta * 0.85)

        if t - last_improve > 2.0 * no_improve_limit:
            break

    # Final symmetrization check.
    a_sym = 0.5 * (cur_a + cur_a[::-1])
    a_sym = _safe_norm(a_sym)
    Ms = max_M(a_sym, L, Nscore)
    if Ms < best_M - 1e-15:
        best_M = Ms
        best_a = a_sym.copy()
        if mode == "sym":
            best_var = a_to_b_sym(best_a, n)
        else:
            best_var = best_a.copy()

    return best_a, best_M


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Low-dimensional smooth parameterization
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def make_features(n, total_d):
    """Chebyshev basis, orthonormalized under the uniform measure and RMS-normalized."""
    if total_d <= 1:
        return np.ones((n, 1), dtype=np.float64)

    x = (2.0 * np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n - 1)) - 1.0

    raws = []
    T0 = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    raws.append(T0.copy())

    T1 = x.copy()
    raws.append(T1.copy())
    for _ in range(2, total_d):
        T2 = 2.0 * x * T1 - T0
        raws.append(T2.copy())
        T0, T1 = T1, T2

    cols = []
    for c in raws:
        v = c.astype(np.float64).copy()
        for u in cols:
            coeff = float(np.dot(u, v) / n)
            v -= coeff * u
        norm = float(np.linalg.norm(v))
        if norm <= 1e-12 * n:
            continue
        v = v / (norm / math.sqrt(n))
        cols.append(v)

    if not cols:
        cols.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    return np.column_stack(cols)


def param_to_a(theta, F):
    """a = softmax(F @ theta)."""
    phi = F @ theta
    phi -= float(phi.max())
    a = np.exp(phi)
    return _safe_norm(a)


def param_initial_theta(a, F):
    """Least-squares projection of log(a) onto feature space."""
    y = np.log(_safe_norm(a) + 1e-300)
    A = F.T @ F
    b = F.T @ y
    d = F.shape[1]
    A = A + 1e-6 * np.eye(d, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.linalg.solve(A, b)


def optimize_params(a0, n, info, F, theta0, budget, deadline, rng, label=""):
    """Low-dimensional mirror-style line search over theta in a = softmax(F theta)."""
    L, Nscore, Ngrad, idx = info
    budget = max(5.0, float(budget))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    end = min(float(deadline), t0 + budget)

    theta = np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    cur_a = param_to_a(theta, F)
    cur_M = max_M(cur_a, L, Nscore)

    best_a = cur_a.copy()
    best_M = cur_M
    best_theta = theta.copy()

    step = 3.0
    fail = 0
    last_improve = t0
    last_print = t0
    d = F.shape[1]

    while time.monotonic() < end:
        t = time.monotonic()
        if t >= end:
            break

        progress = min(1.0, (t - t0) / budget)
        K = int(min(L, max(64, L * (0.03 + 0.32 * (progress ** 0.6)))))
        smooth_alpha = 0.5 - 0.4 * progress

        C, Ff = compute_C_F(cur_a, Ngrad, L)
        M = float(C.max())

        if M < best_M - 1e-15:
            best_M = M
            best_a = cur_a.copy()
            best_theta = theta.copy()
            last_improve = t
            if t - last_print > 5.0:
                print(f"    {label}: param best M={M:.10f} score={2.0 * n * M:.7f}")
                last_print = t

        W = make_weights(C, M, K, smooth_alpha)
        Ga = gradient_from_W(W, Ff, Ngrad, idx)

        ag = cur_a * Ga
        sumag = float(ag.sum())
        ma = F.T @ cur_a
        ba = F.T @ ag
        grad_theta = ba - sumag * ma

        normg = float(np.linalg.norm(grad_theta))
        if not math.isfinite(normg) or normg < 1e-14:
            break

        direction = grad_theta / normg
        accepted = False
        candidates = (step, 0.5 * step, 0.25 * step)

        for s in candidates:
            th = theta - s * direction
            at = param_to_a(th, F)
            Mtry = max_M(at, L, Nscore)
            if math.isfinite(Mtry) and Mtry < cur_M - 1e-12:
                accepted = True
                theta = th
                cur_a = at
                cur_M = Mtry
                fail = 0

                if s == candidates[0]:
                    step = min(5.0, step * 1.02)
                else:
                    step = float(s)

                if Mtry < best_M - 1e-15:
                    best_M = Mtry
                    best_a = at.copy()
                    best_theta = theta.copy()
                    last_improve = t
                    if t - last_print > 5.0:
                        print(f"    {label}: param best M={Mtry:.10f} score={2.0 * n * Mtry:.7f}")
                        last_print = t
                break

        if not accepted:
            fail += 1
            step = max(0.05, step * 0.8)
            if fail >= 8:
                if rng.random() < 0.5:
                    theta = theta + rng.normal(0.0, 0.4, d)
                else:
                    theta = best_theta.copy()
                    theta = theta + rng.normal(0.0, 0.2, d)
                cur_a = param_to_a(theta, F)
                cur_M = max_M(cur_a, L, Nscore)
                step = 2.5
                fail = 0

        if t - last_improve > 75.0:
            break

    a_sym = 0.5 * (best_a + best_a[::-1])
    a_sym = _safe_norm(a_sym)
    Ms = max_M(a_sym, L, Nscore)
    if Ms < best_M - 1e-15:
        best_M = Ms
        best_a = a_sym.copy()

    return best_a, best_M, best_theta


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Frequency-domain nonnegative starts
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def random_smooth_sequence(n, rng):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    y = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for _ in range(3):
        freq = int(rng.integers(1, 6))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.10, 0.55))
        y += amp * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * freq * x + phase)
    y = np.maximum(y, 1e-3)
    return _safe_norm(y)


def spectral_constmag_start(n, rng, iters=90, beta=0.6, sym=True):
    """
    Project onto nonnegative time domain while flattening non-DC Fourier magnitude.
    """
    a = random_smooth_sequence(n, rng)
    if sym:
        a = 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])
        a = _safe_norm(a)

    N = 1 << max(1, (4 * n - 1).bit_length())

    for _ in range(max(1, int(iters))):
        F = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        mag = np.abs(F)
        mag_safe = np.maximum(mag, 1e-300)

        target = mag_safe.copy()
        if target.size > 1:
            mu = float(np.mean(mag[1:]))
            if not math.isfinite(mu) or mu <= 0.0:
                mu = 1.0
            target[1:] = (mag_safe[1:] ** beta) * (mu ** (1.0 - beta))

        F2 = F * (target / mag_safe)
        new = np.fft.irfft(F2, N)[:n]

        if sym:
            new = 0.5 * (new + new[::-1])
        new = np.maximum(new, 0.0)

        s = float(new.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-8:
            break
        a = new / s

    return a


def generate_starts(base, n, info, rng):
    """Create a set of diverse normalized nonnegative starting sequences."""
    starts = []
    L, Nscore, Ngrad, idx = info

    add_start(starts, "base", base, info)
    add_start(starts, "base_sym", 0.5 * (base + base[::-1]), info)
    add_start(starts, "base_hann_shift",
              base * (0.25 + 0.75 * (0.5 - 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * np.linspace(0, 1, n)))),
              info)

    j = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    c1 = np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * j)
    c2 = np.cos(4.0 * math.pi * j)

    add_start(starts, "uniform", np.ones(n), info)
    add_start(starts, "hann", 0.5 - 0.5 * c1, info)
    add_start(starts, "hamming", 0.54 - 0.46 * c1, info)
    add_start(starts, "blackman", np.maximum(0.42 - 0.5 * c1 + 0.08 * c2, 1e-6), info)

    for w, tag in [(0.08, "g08"), (0.15, "g15"), (0.25, "g25"), (0.40, "g40")]:
        add_start(starts, tag, np.exp(-((j - 0.5) / w) ** 2) + 1e-4, info)

    bimodal = (np.exp(-((j - 0.22) / 0.09) ** 2)
               + np.exp(-((j - 0.78) / 0.09) ** 2) + 1e-3)
    add_start(starts, "bimodal", bimodal, info)
    add_start(starts, "asym_peak", np.exp(-((j - 0.62) / 0.23) ** 2) + 1e-3, info)
    add_start(starts, "asym_peak2", np.exp(-((j - 0.38) / 0.19) ** 2) + 1e-3, info)
    add_start(starts, "ramp_l", np.linspace(0.02, 1.0, n), info)
    add_start(starts, "ramp_r", np.linspace(1.0, 0.02, n), info)

    for _ in range(3):
        add_start(starts, "rand_smooth", random_smooth_sequence(n, rng), info)

    for beta_val in (0.35, 0.55, 0.75):
        add_start(starts, f"spect_beta_{beta_val}",
                  spectral_constmag_start(n, rng, iters=90, beta=beta_val, sym=True),
                  info)

    return starts


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Exact pair-transfer polish
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def pair_eval(C, lin, d, ii, jj, kij):
    """Max coefficient after moving delta d: C + d*lin + quadratic impulses."""
    tmp = C + d * lin
    dq = d * d
    tmp[ii] += dq
    tmp[jj] += dq
    tmp[kij] -= 2.0 * dq
    return float(np.max(tmp))


def pair_polish(a0, info, deadline, rng, passes=300, top_select=10):
    """
    Greedy exact two-coordinate moves.
    For a move from i to j, the change of conv(a,a) is quadratic and can be
    evaluated exactly in O(n) without a full FFT.
    """
    L, Nscore, Ngrad, idx = info
    n = len(a0)
    a = _safe_norm(a0)
    C, F = compute_C_F(a, Ngrad, L)
    M = float(C.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = M

    fracs = (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.50, 0.70, 1.00)
    no_imp = 0

    for _ in range(passes):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break

        K = max(64, L // 8)
        W = make_weights(C, M, K, 0.15)
        G = gradient_from_W(W, F, Ngrad, idx)

        d_idx = np.argpartition(G, -top_select)[-top_select:]
        r_idx = np.argpartition(G, top_select)
        rand_idx = rng.integers(0, n, size=top_select)

        donor_list = np.unique(np.concatenate((d_idx, rand_idx))).astype(np.int64)
        recv_list = np.unique(np.concatenate((r_idx, rand_idx))).astype(np.int64)
        donor_list = donor_list[:top_select + 2]
        recv_list = recv_list[:top_select + 2]

        improved = False

        for ii in donor_list:
            i = int(ii)
            for jj in recv_list:
                j = int(jj)
                if i == j:
                    continue

                ai = float(a[i])
                aj = float(a[j])
                if ai + aj <= 1e-18:
                    continue

                lin = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
                lin[j:j + n] += 2.0 * a
                lin[i:i + n] -= 2.0 * a

                ii_idx = 2 * i
                jj_idx = 2 * j
                kij_idx = i + j

                best_val = M
                best_delta = 0.0

                for frac in fracs:
                    d = frac * ai
                    if d > 1e-18:
                        val = pair_eval(C, lin, d, ii_idx, jj_idx, kij_idx)
                        if val < best_val:
                            best_val = val
                            best_delta = d

                    d = -frac * aj
                    if d < -1e-18:
                        val = pair_eval(C, lin, d, ii_idx, jj_idx, kij_idx)
                        if val < best_val:
                            best_val = val
                            best_delta = d

                if best_delta != 0.0 and best_val < M - 1e-13:
                    a[i] -= best_delta
                    a[j] += best_delta
                    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
                    s = float(a.sum())
                    if s > 0.0:
                        a /= s

                    C, F = compute_C_F(a, Ngrad, L)
                    M = float(C.max())
                    improved = True
                    if M < best_M:
                        best_M = M
                        best_a = a.copy()

        if improved:
            no_imp = 0
        else:
            no_imp += 1
            if no_imp >= 12:
                break

    a_sym = 0.5 * (best_a + best_a[::-1])
    a_sym = _safe_norm(a_sym)
    Ms = max_M(a_sym, L, Nscore)
    if Ms < best_M:
        best_M = Ms
        best_a = a_sym.copy()

    return best_a, best_M


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main search entry point
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for a nonnegative sequence minimizing the official evaluation.
    """
    seed = int(seed)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t_start + max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 25.0)

    # Current best from previous constructions / global variable.
    try:
        base = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    except Exception:
        base = np.ones(6121, dtype=np.float64)

    base = np.clip(base, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if base.size == 0:
        base = np.ones(6121, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(base.size)
    base = _safe_norm(base)
    info = make_info(n)
    L, Nscore, Ngrad, idx = info

    global_best_a = base.copy()
    global_best_M = max_M(global_best_a, L, Nscore)
    global_best_score = 2.0 * n * global_best_M
    print(f"Base n={n}, initial M={global_best_M:.10f}, score={global_best_score:.7f}")

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Diverse starts.
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    starts = generate_starts(base, n, info, rng)
    starts.sort(key=start_sort_key)

    for Mv, name, _ in starts[:10]:
        print(f"  start {name:18s} score={2.0 * n * Mv:.7f}")

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Low-dimensional parametric exploration.
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    if budget_s > 1800:
        param_time = min(180.0, max(90.0, 0.08 * budget_s))
        param_end = time.monotonic() + param_time
        param_end = min(param_end, deadline - 1200.0)

        if param_end > time.monotonic() + 60.0:
            print("\n=== Parametric exploration ===")

            F16 = make_features(n, 16)
            theta0 = param_initial_theta(global_best_a, F16)

            specs = [
                ("p16a", theta0 + rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, 16)),
                ("p16b", theta0 + rng.normal(0.0, 0.80, 16)),
                ("p16r", rng.normal(0.0, 1.00, 16)),
            ]

            for name, th in specs:
                if time.monotonic() > param_end - 15.0:
                    break
                a_opt, M_opt, _ = optimize_params(
                    global_best_a, n, info, F16, th,
                    budget=45.0, deadline=param_end, rng=rng, label=name
                )
                add_start(starts, name, a_opt, info)
                if M_opt < global_best_M:
                    global_best_M = M_opt
                    global_best_a = a_opt.copy()
                    global_best_score = 2.0 * n * M_opt
                    print(f"  {name}: new best score={global_best_score:.7f}")

            F24 = make_features(n, 24)
            if time.monotonic() < param_end - 30.0:
                th24 = param_initial_theta(starts[0][2], F24) + rng.normal(0.0, 0.50, 24)
                a_opt, M_opt, _ = optimize_params(
                    starts[0][2], n, info, F24, th24,
                    budget=60.0, deadline=param_end, rng=rng, label="p24"
                )
                add_start(starts, "p24", a_opt, info)
                if M_opt < global_best_M:
                    global_best_M = M_opt
                    global_best_a = a_opt.copy()
                    global_best_score = 2.0 * n * M_opt
                    print(f"  p24: new best score={global_best_score:.7f}")

            starts.sort(key=start_sort_key)
            if starts[0][0] < global_best_M:
                global_best_M = starts[0][0]
                global_best_a = starts[0][2].copy()
                global_best_score = 2.0 * n * global_best_M

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Local refinements from the best starts.
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    final_reserve = min(520.0, max(160.0, 0.22 * budget_s))
    if deadline - final_reserve <= time.monotonic() + 120.0:
        final_reserve = 120.0

    local_deadline = min(deadline - 20.0, deadline - final_reserve)

    print("\n=== Local mirror descent ===")

    full_budgets = (420.0, 270.0, 180.0, 120.0, 90.0, 65.0, 50.0, 35.0, 25.0, 15.0)
    sym_budgets = (170.0, 95.0, 65.0, 45.0, 30.0, 20.0, 15.0, 10.0, 5.0, 5.0)

    max_starts = min(10, len(starts))
    for rank in range(max_starts):
        rem = local_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 30.0:
            break

        M0, name, a0 = starts[rank]
        tf = min(full_budgets[rank], max(20.0, 0.72 * rem))
        if tf < 20.0:
            break

        a_opt, M_opt = local_descent(
            a0, n, info, "full", tf, local_deadline, rng,
            label=f"full{rank}_{name[:12]}",
            no_improve_limit=60.0 if rank < 2 else 35.0
        )
        if M_opt < global_best_M:
            global_best_M = M_opt
            global_best_a = a_opt.copy()
            global_best_score = 2.0 * n * M_opt
            print(f"  full rank {rank}: new best score={global_best_score:.7f}")

        rem = local_deadline - time.monotonic()
        ts = min(sym_budgets[rank], 0.45 * rem)
        if ts < 15.0:
            continue

        a_opt, M_opt = local_descent(
            a0, n, info, "sym", ts, local_deadline, rng,
            label=f"sym{rank}_{name[:12]}",
            no_improve_limit=40.0 if rank < 2 else 25.0
        )
        if M_opt < global_best_M:
            global_best_M = M_opt
            global_best_a = a_opt.copy()
            global_best_score = 2.0 * n * M_opt
            print(f"  sym rank {rank}: new best score={global_best_score:.7f}")

    # Use any remaining local budget from the current global best.
    mode = "full"
    while time.monotonic() < local_deadline - 40.0:
        rem = local_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 45.0:
            break
        tf = min(120.0, rem - 35.0)
        if tf < 30.0:
            break
        a_opt, M_opt = local_descent(
            global_best_a, n, info, mode, tf, local_deadline, rng,
            label=f"loop_{mode}",
            no_improve_limit=30.0
        )
        if M_opt < global_best_M:
            global_best_M = M_opt
            global_best_a = a_opt.copy()
            global_best_score = 2.0 * n * M_opt
            print(f"  loop {mode}: new best score={global_best_score:.7f}")
        mode = "sym" if mode == "full" else "full"

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Final polish.
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    print("\n=== Final polish ===")

    # Pair-transfer polish.
    pair_deadline = min(deadline - 90.0, time.monotonic() + min(240.0, max(90.0, 0.20 * budget_s)))
    if pair_deadline > time.monotonic() + 30.0:
        a_pol, M_pol = pair_polish(global_best_a, info, pair_deadline, rng,
                                   passes=500, top_select=10)
        if M_pol < global_best_M:
            global_best_M = M_pol
            global_best_a = a_pol.copy()
            global_best_score = 2.0 * n * M_pol
            print(f"  pair polish: new best score={global_best_score:.7f}")

    # Short symmetric continuation.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 150.0:
        a_opt, M_opt = local_descent(
            global_best_a, n, info, "sym", min(110.0, rem - 80.0),
            deadline - 40.0, rng, label="final_sym", no_improve_limit=25.0
        )
        if M_opt < global_best_M:
            global_best_M = M_opt
            global_best_a = a_opt.copy()
            global_best_score = 2.0 * n * M_opt
            print(f"  final sym: new best score={global_best_score:.7f}")

    # Very last micro local full pass.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 90.0:
        a_opt, M_opt = local_descent(
            global_best_a, n, info, "full", min(70.0, rem - 40.0),
            deadline - 25.0, rng, label="final_full_micro", no_improve_limit=20.0
        )
        if M_opt < global_best_M:
            global_best_M = M_opt
            global_best_a = a_opt.copy()
            global_best_score = 2.0 * n * M_opt
            print(f"  final full micro: new best score={global_best_score:.7f}")

    # One more pair polish if enough time remains.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 80.0:
        a_pol, M_pol = pair_polish(global_best_a, info, deadline - 12.0, rng,
                                   passes=400, top_select=8)
        if M_pol < global_best_M:
            global_best_M = M_pol
            global_best_a = a_pol.copy()
            global_best_score = 2.0 * n * M_pol
            print(f"  final pair polish: new best score={global_best_score:.7f}")

    # Final sym check.
    a_sym = 0.5 * (global_best_a + global_best_a[::-1])
    a_sym = _safe_norm(a_sym)
    Ms = max_M(a_sym, L, Nscore)
    if Ms < global_best_M:
        global_best_M = Ms
        global_best_a = a_sym.copy()
        global_best_score = 2.0 * n * Ms
        print(f"  final sym check: new best score={global_best_score:.7f}")

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. Validate with official evaluator and return.
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    best_f = np.clip(np.asarray(global_best_a, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(best_f.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        best_f = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(best_f.sum())
    best_f = best_f / s

    official_score = np.inf
    try:
        official_score = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_f])
    except Exception as exc:
        print("Official evaluation failed:", exc)
        official_score = np.inf

    if not math.isfinite(official_score):
        fallback = _safe_norm(base)
        fallback_seq = [float(x) for x in fallback]
        try:
            official_score = evaluate_sequence(fallback_seq)
            if math.isfinite(official_score):
                best_f = fallback
        except Exception:
            best_f = fallback

    print(f"\nBest fast score: {global_best_score:.7f}")
    print(f"Official score: {official_score}")
    print(f"Time used: {time.monotonic() - t_start:.1f}s")

    return [float(x) for x in np.clip(best_f, 0.0, 1000.0)]
```
